  2036 | void NavigationRuntimeNode::onGoal(
  2037 |     const navigation_contracts::msg::NavigationGoal::ConstSharedPtr& message) {
  2038 |   if (!message) {
  2039 |     RCLCPP_ERROR(get_logger(), "rejected null navigation goal");
  2040 |     return;
  2041 |   }
  2042 |   const auto route = decodeRouteSnapshot(*message);
  2043 |   const bool route_mirrors_valid =
  2044 |       route.has_value() && routeSnapshotMatchesGoalMirrors(*route, *message);
  2045 |   if (!route.has_value() || !route_mirrors_valid) {
  2046 |     // This message was never accepted as a lifecycle event.  It must not
  2047 |     // revoke a certified active command or a newer pending goal.
  2048 |     RCLCPP_ERROR(get_logger(), "rejected navigation goal: immutable route snapshot is invalid");
  2049 |     return;
  2050 |   }
  2051 |   {
  2052 |     std::unique_lock<std::mutex> localization_lock(localization_transition_mutex_);
  2053 |     std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(input_mutex_);
  2054 |     applyValidatedGoalLocked(message);
  2055 |   }
  2056 |         if (consumeForeignMissionCancelIfCurrent() && planning_worker_) {
  2057 |           planning_worker_->cancelActive();
  2058 |         }
  2059 | }

  2061 | void NavigationRuntimeNode::transitionForeignMissionLocked(
  2062 |     const bool defer_until_certified_stop) {
  2063 |   // This helper is called only with input_mutex_ -> transitionMutex() held.
  2064 |   // A foreign mission is not ordered by request_id: it is a control-authority
  2065 |   // violation.  While moving, preserve the certified suffix and defer the
  2066 |   // same complete transition until measured stop; otherwise fail closed now.
  2067 |   if (defer_until_certified_stop) {
  2068 |     foreign_mission_hold_after_stop_ = true;
  2069 |     pending_goal_owner_.clearGoal();
  2070 |     deferred_terminal_status_.reset();
  2071 |     return;
  2072 |   }
  2073 |   // The worker interrupt is deliberately post-action: cancelActive() may
  2074 |   // enter backend code and must not run while lifecycle locks are held.
  2075 |   foreign_cancel_target_epoch_ = active_goal_epoch_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
  2076 |   foreign_cancel_localization_epoch_ =
  2077 |       active_localization_epoch_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
  2078 |   foreign_mission_cancel_pending_.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
  2079 |   pending_goal_owner_.clearGoal();
  2080 |   deferred_terminal_status_.reset();
  2081 |   foreign_mission_hold_after_stop_ = false;
  2082 |   const auto next_goal_epoch = advanceMonotonicId(active_goal_epoch_);
  2083 |   if (next_goal_epoch) {
  2084 |     foreign_cancel_transition_epoch_ = *next_goal_epoch;
  2085 |     (void)command_bundle_store_.setActiveGoalEpoch(*next_goal_epoch, false);

…

  2086 |   } else {
  2087 |     command_bundle_store_.invalidate();
  2088 |   }
  2089 |   active_goal_.reset();
  2090 |   executing_goal_.reset();
  2091 |   command_bundle_store_.invalidate();
  2092 |   new_goal_ = false;
  2093 |   hot_goal_transition_ = false;
  2094 |   execution_episode_.clearRestartFromRest();
  2095 |   failClosedLocked();
  2096 |   mission_start_position_world_.reset();
  2097 |   mission_start_mission_id_.clear();
  2098 |   mission_start_route_revision_ = 0U;
  2099 |   mission_start_localization_epoch_ = 0U;

    23 | class PendingGoalHandoffOwner {
    24 |  public:
    25 |   using GoalConstPtr = navigation_contracts::msg::NavigationGoal::ConstSharedPtr;
    26 | 
    27 |   // The owner stores one immutable message, not a parallel identity and
    28 |   // message optional.  The caller must hold the runtime input/transition
    29 |   // transaction while deciding suffix ownership and invoking this method.
    30 |   bool enqueueGoal(const GoalConstPtr& candidate,
    31 |                   const std::optional<navigation_contracts::msg::NavigationGoal>& active,

   179 |     if (active_) {

   181 |       // See submit(): this backend operation must be in the same lifecycle
   182 |       // critical section as the active-item identity it cancels.

   194 |       const std::uint64_t localization_epoch,

   203 |     // clears active_ only after its backend call returns, and submit() also

   211 |         active_->key.committed_bundle_generation !=
   212 |             committed_bundle_generation) {
   213 |       return false;
   214 |     }

   283 |             std::chrono::microseconds>(worker_started_at - work->enqueued_at).count();

   285 |         active_->key = work->key;
   286 |         active_->priority = work->priority;
   287 |         job_stop = active_->stop_source.get_token();
   288 |         snapshot_.pending = false;
   289 |         snapshot_.in_flight = true;
   290 |         ++snapshot_.started;
   291 |       }

   305 |         }

   309 |           std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
   310 |           failure_ = failure;

   330 |         ++snapshot_.completed;
   331 |       }
   332 |       cv_.notify_all();
   333 |     }

   255 |   // Stage, but do not expose, a complete successor. The transaction watermark
   256 |   // is consumed at staging so an older result cannot overwrite a newer

   269 |     }

   346 |   // The command timer calls this operation before sampling. No callback or
   347 |   // planner code can replace active outside this single atomic boundary.
   348 |   // The exact timeline snapshot is a transaction token. Its version,
   349 |   // pending pointer/generation and activation instant must still match under
   350 |   // the store mutex before any activation or rollback is attempted.
   351 |   template <typename FinalizeFn>
   352 |   bool activatePendingIfDueAndFinalize(
   353 |       std::int64_t now_ns, const ExecutionTimelineSnapshot& expected,
   354 |       FinalizeFn&& finalize) const noexcept {
   355 |     std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);

   357 |         now_ns, expected.pending, expected.version, expected.pending_activation_ns,
   358 |         true, std::forward<FinalizeFn>(finalize));
   359 |   }
   360 | 
   361 |   // Execute the exposure callback while the same transaction lock protects
   362 |   // the committed bundle, goal epoch and world identity. A sampler may have
   363 |   // loaded a shared_ptr just before a map update invalidated it; pointer and
   364 |   // identity revalidation at this boundary prevents that stale command from
   365 |   // reaching the transport. The callback is intentionally inside the lock so
   366 |   // invalidation cannot complete before an already-authorized exposure; the
   367 |   // caller must keep this callback bounded because it serializes store writes.
   368 |   // Returning false rejects exposure at this exact linearization point; a
   369 |   // freshness or transport lease checked before waiting for this mutex is not
   370 |   // sufficient authorization.

    35 | // The sampler evaluates an immutable bundle. It does not acquire world locks,
    36 | // call ROS, perform trajectory validation, or finalize planner state. The

    16 | enum class ExecutionRecoveryEvent : std::uint8_t {

    18 |   kBackupActivated,
    19 |   kEmergencyCommitted,
    20 |   kCertifiedStopObserved,
    21 |   // A nominal terminal STOP is a distinct completion event. A safety
    22 |   // endpoint must never be promoted by completion geometry alone.
    23 |   kTerminalStopCompleted,
    24 |   kEmergencyCertificationFailed,

    28 |     ExecutionRecoveryState state) noexcept {
    29 |   return state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kInitialHold ||
    30 |          state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kTrackMain ||
    31 |          state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kTrackBackup ||

    32 |          state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kEmergencyBrake ||
    33 |          state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kStoppedRecovery ||
    34 |          state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kPx4Hold;
    35 | }
    36 | 
    37 | [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool nominalPlanningAllowed(
    38 |     ExecutionRecoveryState state) noexcept {
    39 |   return state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kInitialHold ||
    40 |          state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kTrackMain ||
    41 |          state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kStoppedRecovery;
    42 | }

…

    43 | 
    44 | // BACKUP and emergency braking are one-way until a fresh measured stop is
    45 | // observed. In particular, a late nominal result cannot replace either moving
    46 | // safety command. The caller owns freshness and the <=0.15 m/s measurement;
    47 | // this transition function owns only the public state semantics.
    48 | [[nodiscard]] constexpr ExecutionRecoveryState transitionExecutionRecovery(
    49 |     ExecutionRecoveryState state, ExecutionRecoveryEvent event) noexcept {
    50 |   if (!executionRecoveryStateKnown(state)) {
    51 |     return ExecutionRecoveryState::kPx4Hold;
    52 |   }
    53 |   if (state == ExecutionRecoveryState::kPx4Hold) {
    54 |     return state;
    55 |   }
    56 |   if (event == ExecutionRecoveryEvent::kEmergencyCertificationFailed) {
    57 |     return ExecutionRecoveryState::kPx4Hold;
    58 |   }
    59 |   switch (state) {
    60 |     case ExecutionRecoveryState::kInitialHold:
    61 |       return event == ExecutionRecoveryEvent::kMainCommitted
    62 |           ? ExecutionRecoveryState::kTrackMain : state;
    63 |     case ExecutionRecoveryState::kTrackMain:
    64 |       if (event == ExecutionRecoveryEvent::kBackupActivated) {
    65 |         return ExecutionRecoveryState::kTrackBackup;
    66 |       }
    67 |       if (event == ExecutionRecoveryEvent::kEmergencyCommitted) {

…

    68 |         return ExecutionRecoveryState::kEmergencyBrake;
    69 |       }
    70 |       if (event == ExecutionRecoveryEvent::kTerminalStopCompleted) {
    71 |         return ExecutionRecoveryState::kStoppedRecovery;
    72 |       }
    73 |       return state;
    74 |     case ExecutionRecoveryState::kTrackBackup:
    75 |     case ExecutionRecoveryState::kEmergencyBrake:
    76 |       return event == ExecutionRecoveryEvent::kCertifiedStopObserved
    77 |           ? ExecutionRecoveryState::kStoppedRecovery : state;
    78 |     case ExecutionRecoveryState::kStoppedRecovery:
    79 |       return event == ExecutionRecoveryEvent::kMainCommitted
    80 |           ? ExecutionRecoveryState::kTrackMain : state;
    81 |     case ExecutionRecoveryState::kPx4Hold:
    82 |       return state;
    83 |   }
    84 |   return ExecutionRecoveryState::kPx4Hold;
    85 | }

    41 | struct ExecutionEpisodeSnapshot final {

   190 |   bool stoppedHold(
   191 |       const navigation_planning::CandidateBundle& bundle) noexcept {
   192 |     std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
   193 |     if (state_.failure_latched || !activeBundleIdentityMatches(bundle)) return false;
   194 |     // A stopped hold is also the command publisher's representation while a
   195 |     // measured-state PlanFromRest retry is in flight.  Do not erase that
   196 |     // lifecycle request from the 50 Hz hold samples; otherwise the next
   197 |     // non-zero odometry sample is misclassified as motion without an
   198 |     // authorized recovery and the node falls through to PX4 Hold.
   199 |     const bool restart_requested = state_.restart_from_rest;
   200 |     state_.active_generation = bundle.bundle_generation;
   201 |     state_.phase = ExecutionEpisodePhase::kStoppedHold;
   202 |     state_.command_available = true;
   203 |     state_.safety_suffix_active = false;
   204 |     state_.restart_from_rest = restart_requested;
   205 |     return true;

   206 |   }
   207 | 
   208 |   void failClosed() noexcept {
   209 |     std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
   210 |     state_.phase = ExecutionEpisodePhase::kPx4Hold;
   211 |     state_.command_available = false;
   212 |     state_.failure_latched = true;
   213 |     state_.safety_suffix_active = false;
   214 |     state_.restart_from_rest = false;
   215 |     state_.recovery_state = ExecutionRecoveryState::kPx4Hold;
   216 |   }
   217 | 
   218 |   void suspendCommand() noexcept {
   219 |     std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
   220 |     state_.command_available = false;
   221 |   }
   222 | 
   223 |   void clearGoal(std::uint64_t localization_epoch) noexcept {
   224 |     std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
   225 |     state_ = {};
   226 |     state_.localization_epoch = localization_epoch;
   227 |     state_.phase = ExecutionEpisodePhase::kInitialHold;
   228 |     state_.recovery_state = ExecutionRecoveryState::kInitialHold;
   229 |   }
   230 | 

…

   231 |  private:
   232 |   [[nodiscard]] bool bundleIdentityMatchesEpisode(
   233 |       const navigation_planning::CandidateBundle& bundle) const noexcept {
   234 |     return bundle.localization_epoch != 0U && bundle.goal_epoch != 0U &&
   235 |            bundle.request_id != 0U && bundle.bundle_generation != 0U &&
   236 |            state_.localization_epoch == bundle.localization_epoch &&
   237 |            state_.goal_epoch == bundle.goal_epoch &&
   238 |            state_.request_id == bundle.request_id;
   239 |   }
   240 | 
   241 |   [[nodiscard]] bool activeBundleIdentityMatches(
   242 |       const navigation_planning::CandidateBundle& bundle) const noexcept {
   243 |     return bundleIdentityMatchesEpisode(bundle) &&
   244 |            state_.active_generation == bundle.bundle_generation;
   245 |   }
   246 | 
   247 |   mutable std::mutex mutex_;
   248 |   ExecutionEpisodeSnapshot state_{};
   249 | };

    73 | void MissionController::deactivate() {
    74 |   std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    75 |   if (state_ != MissionControllerState::Complete &&
    76 |       state_ != MissionControllerState::Failed) {
    77 |     checkpoint_valid_ = true;
    78 |   }
    79 |   state_ = MissionControllerState::Idle;
    80 |   braking_start_time_s_.reset();
    81 |   braking_end_time_s_.reset();
    82 |   stopped_start_time_s_.reset();
    83 |   arrival_start_time_s_.reset();
    84 |   pending_position_control_ = false;
    85 |   trajectory_ready_ = false;
    86 |   terminal_hold_pending_ = false;
    87 |   previous_position_.reset();

  1477 | void NavigationMode::handleMissionEvent(const MissionControllerEvent& event, double now_s) {
  1478 |   if (!mission_controller_ || event.type == MissionControllerEvent::Type::None) return;
  1479 |   if (event.type == MissionControllerEvent::Type::PublishGoal) {
  1480 |     const auto waypoint = mission_controller_->activeWaypoint();
  1481 |     if (!waypoint.has_value()) {
  1482 |       handover_requested_ = true;
  1483 |       return;
  1484 |     }
  1485 |     {
  1486 |       std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(trajectory_mutex_);
  1487 |       const auto route = mission_controller_->routeSnapshot();
  1488 |       const bool terminal_successor =
  1489 |           navigation_mission::stopHasCoincidentPassThroughPredecessor(route);
  1490 |       // Waypoint acceptance and planner publication run on independent
  1491 |       // callbacks. Preserve the exact old accepted command under its old
  1492 |       // identity until a new command is committed atomically. Never relabel it
  1493 |       // here; normal validity/freshness expiry remains fail-closed.
  1494 |       if (navigation_command_.has_value() &&
  1495 |           commandMayBeRetainedAcrossWaypointHandoff(
  1496 |               *navigation_command_, terminal_successor)) {
  1497 |         ++waypoint_handoff_retained_command_count_;
  1498 |         const auto& retained = *navigation_command_;
  1499 |         safety_suffix_handoff_pending_ =
  1500 |             retained.role == navigation_contracts::msg::NavigationCommand::ROLE_BACKUP &&
  1501 |             (retained.status == navigation_contracts::msg::NavigationCommand::STATUS_READY ||

…

  1502 |              retained.status == navigation_contracts::msg::NavigationCommand::STATUS_COMPLETED);
  1503 |         if (safety_suffix_handoff_pending_) {
  1504 |           safety_suffix_waypoint_index_ = retained.waypoint_index;
  1505 |           safety_suffix_request_id_ = retained.request_id;
  1506 |         }
  1507 |       } else {
  1508 |         // A rejected command is a terminal status for the old waypoint, not a
  1509 |         // accepted command that may bridge the waypoint handoff.  If it is
  1510 |         // retained here, the mission timer can publish the next goal and the
  1511 |         // setpoint callback can immediately consume the old rejection before
  1512 |         // the runtime has processed that goal, causing a false PX4 Hold.
  1513 |         navigation_command_ = transitionCertifiedCommand(
  1514 |             navigation_command_, std::nullopt,
  1515 |             CertifiedCommandTransition::kInvalidate);
  1516 |         safety_suffix_handoff_pending_ = false;
  1517 |         safety_suffix_waypoint_index_ = 0U;
  1518 |         safety_suffix_request_id_ = 0U;
  1519 |       }

  2894 | void NavigationModeExecutor::schedulePx4Hold(bool complete_navigation_failure) {
  2895 |   hold_handover_pending_ = true;

  2904 |   });

  2907 | void NavigationModeExecutor::onPx4HoldHandoverCompleted(
  2908 |     px4_ros2::Result result, bool complete_navigation_failure) {

  2910 |     hold_handover_in_flight_ = false;
  2911 |     hold_handover_pending_ = false;
  2912 |     RCLCPP_INFO(node_.get_logger(), "PX4 Hold handover completed with result=%s",
  2913 |                 px4_ros2::resultToString(result));
  2914 |     return;
  2915 |   }
  2916 | 
  2917 |   hold_handover_in_flight_ = false;
  2918 |   hold_handover_pending_ = true;
  2919 |   hold_handover_complete_navigation_failure_ =
  2920 |       hold_handover_complete_navigation_failure_ || complete_navigation_failure;
  2921 |   constexpr std::int64_t kHoldRetryPeriodNs = 250'000'000LL;
  2922 |   hold_handover_next_retry_steady_ns_ =
  2923 |       navigation_common::steadyClockNowNanoseconds() + kHoldRetryPeriodNs;
  2924 |   RCLCPP_ERROR_THROTTLE(
  2925 |       node_.get_logger(), *node_.get_clock(), 5000,
  2926 |       "PX4 Hold handover attempt=%u failed with result=%s; keeping the explicit "
  2927 |       "stationary safety stream and retrying until AUTO_LOITER is confirmed",
  2928 |       hold_handover_attempts_, px4_ros2::resultToString(result));
  2929 | }

    21 | inline bool commandValidAt(
    22 |     const NavigationCommand& command, std::int64_t now_ns) noexcept {
    23 |   if (now_ns <= 0) return false;

    25 |   const auto valid_until_ns = commandStampNanoseconds(command.valid_until);

    27 |          now_ns <= valid_until_ns;

    30 | // Pure lease predicate shared by the PX4 update path and its focused tests.
    31 | // All inputs are sampled by the caller; this function owns no freshness or
    32 | // lifecycle state and fails closed on any missing lease component.
    33 | inline bool continuationWitnessLeaseValid(
    34 |     const bool command_valid_at, const std::int64_t now_ns,
    35 |     const std::int64_t last_receive_ns, const std::int64_t stale_after_ns,
    36 |     const ExecutionStateFreshness& odometry_freshness,
    37 |     const ExecutionStateFreshness& health_freshness, const bool health_matches,
    38 |     const bool failure_reported,

    40 |   return command_valid_at && now_ns > 0 && last_receive_ns > 0 &&
    41 |          stale_after_ns >= 0 && now_ns >= last_receive_ns &&
    42 |          now_ns - last_receive_ns <= stale_after_ns &&

    44 |          !failure_reported &&

    86 | inline bool commandContractValid(
    87 |     const NavigationCommand& command, std::string_view expected_frame) noexcept {
    88 |   const auto header_ns = commandStampNanoseconds(command.header.stamp);
    89 |   const auto valid_until_ns = commandStampNanoseconds(command.valid_until);
    90 |   const auto world_stamp_ns = commandStampNanoseconds(command.world_observation_stamp);
    91 |   const auto state_stamp_ns = commandStampNanoseconds(command.state_source_stamp);
    92 |   const bool rejected = command.status == NavigationCommand::STATUS_REJECTED;
    93 |   const bool normal = command.status == NavigationCommand::STATUS_READY ||
    94 |                       command.status == NavigationCommand::STATUS_COMPLETED;
    95 |   const bool braking = command.status == NavigationCommand::STATUS_BRAKING;
    96 |   const bool pvaj_finite = std::isfinite(command.position.x) &&
    97 |                            std::isfinite(command.position.y) &&
    98 |                            std::isfinite(command.position.z) &&
    99 |                            std::isfinite(command.velocity.x) &&
   100 |                            std::isfinite(command.velocity.y) &&
   101 |                            std::isfinite(command.velocity.z) &&
   102 |                            std::isfinite(command.acceleration.x) &&
   103 |                            std::isfinite(command.acceleration.y) &&
   104 |                            std::isfinite(command.acceleration.z) &&
   105 |                            std::isfinite(command.jerk.x) &&
   106 |                            std::isfinite(command.jerk.y) &&
   107 |                            std::isfinite(command.jerk.z) &&
   108 |                            std::isfinite(command.yaw) &&

…

   109 |                            std::isfinite(command.yaw_rate) &&
   110 |                            std::isfinite(command.trajectory_time_s);
   111 |   const bool role_valid = (normal &&
   112 |                            (command.role == NavigationCommand::ROLE_MAIN ||
   113 |                             command.role == NavigationCommand::ROLE_BACKUP) &&
   114 |                            command.bundle_generation != 0U) ||
   115 |                           (braking && command.role == NavigationCommand::ROLE_EMERGENCY &&
   116 |                            command.bundle_generation != 0U) ||
   117 |                           (rejected && command.role == NavigationCommand::ROLE_EMERGENCY);
   118 |   return !expected_frame.empty() && !command.mission_id.empty() &&
   119 |          command.header.frame_id == expected_frame && header_ns > 0 &&
   120 |          valid_until_ns > header_ns && world_stamp_ns > 0 && state_stamp_ns > 0 &&
   121 |          command.localization_epoch != 0U && command.goal_epoch != 0U &&
   122 |          command.world_generation != 0U && command.world_revision != 0U &&
   123 |          command.sample_id != 0U && command.status != NavigationCommand::STATUS_EMPTY &&
   124 |          (normal || braking || rejected) && role_valid && pvaj_finite &&
   125 |          certifiedMainContinuationFieldsValid(command) &&
   126 |          command.trajectory_time_s >= 0.0;

    10 | // The latest propagated state is one immutable, epoch-tagged lease. A state

    33 |     active_epoch_ = localization_epoch;

    35 |     return true;

    41 |   }

    48 | inline ExperimentalTrackingResult assessExperimentalTracking(
    49 |     const navigation_contracts::TrackingExperimentPolicy& policy,
    50 |     const navigation_planning::CandidateBundle& bundle,
    51 |     const Eigen::Vector3d& position, const Eigen::Vector3d& velocity,
    52 |     std::int64_t now_ns, std::int64_t source_ns, double interval_s,
    53 |     bool fresh, bool body_known_free, bool path_clear) {
    54 |   using navigation_planning::CandidateRole;
    55 |   ExperimentalTrackingResult out;
    56 |   // A terminal STOP is still a MAIN command until the exact endpoint
    57 |   // acceptance/settle gate completes.  Allow the adaptive witness to retain
    58 |   // that moving MAIN command, but never allow a safety-role command through
    59 |   // this path.  Completion remains owned by the endpoint/hold contract in the
    60 |   // runtime node below.
    61 |   const bool terminal_main = bundle.terminal_stop &&
    62 |       bundle.role == CandidateRole::kMain &&
    63 |       bundle.kind != navigation_planning::CandidateBundleKind::kBackupOnly &&
    64 |       bundle.kind != navigation_planning::CandidateBundleKind::kEmergencyBrake;
    65 |   if (!policy.enabled || !policy.valid() || !fresh || !body_known_free || !path_clear ||
    66 |       !bundle.valid() || (!terminal_main && bundle.terminal_stop) ||
    67 |       bundle.role != CandidateRole::kMain ||
    68 |       source_ns <= 0 || source_ns > now_ns || !std::isfinite(interval_s) || interval_s <= 0.0)
    69 |     return out;
    70 |   const long double future_ns = static_cast<long double>(now_ns) + interval_s * 1.0e9L;
    71 |   if (future_ns > std::numeric_limits<std::int64_t>::max()) return out;
    72 |   std::int64_t future_sample_ns = static_cast<std::int64_t>(future_ns);

…

    73 |   double effective_interval_s = interval_s;
    74 |   if (terminal_main && future_sample_ns > bundle.declared_end_ns) {

…

   105 |       same_main_interval = true;
   106 |     }
   107 |   }
   108 |   if (!same_main_interval) return out;
   109 |   out.current = navigation_contracts::assessAdaptiveTracking(
   110 |       policy, position, velocity, source->position_world, source->velocity_world);
   111 |   const double horizon_s = static_cast<double>(now_ns - source_ns) * 1.0e-9 +
   112 |       effective_interval_s;
   113 |   out.predicted = navigation_contracts::assessAdaptiveTracking(
   114 |       policy, position + velocity * horizon_s, velocity,
   115 |       future->position_world, future->velocity_world);
   116 |   out.support_valid = out.current.valid && out.predicted.valid;
   117 |   out.accepted = out.support_valid &&
   118 |       navigation_contracts::experimentPermitsTracking(policy, out.current) &&
   119 |       navigation_contracts::experimentPermitsTracking(policy, out.predicted);
   120 |   out.suppression_used = out.accepted && policy.suppress_braking &&
   121 |       (!out.current.within_limits || !out.predicted.within_limits);
